Th<sub>e pr</sub>i<sub>or</sub> i<sub>s ca</sub>lib<sub>ra</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d b<sub>y a one-</sub>di<sub>mens</sub>i<sub>ona</sub>l <sub>ca</sub>lib<sub>ra</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> f<sub>unc-</sub> ti<sub>on</sub> fitt<sub>e</sub>d <sub>on</sub>l<sub>y on</sub> t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>n</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> OOF <sub>pre</sub>di<sub>c</sub>ti<sub>ons an</sub>d l<sub>a</sub>b<sub>e</sub>l<sub>s:</sub>

\mathcal { L } _ { \mathrm { a n c h o r } } = \sum _ { c \in \{ 0 , 1 \} } \Big [ \big \| \mathbf { u } _ { v _ { \pi ( c ) } } - \mathbf { a } _ { c } \big \| _ { 2 } - \rho _ { \mathrm { a n c } } \Big ] _ { + } ^ { 2 } .

## Proxy Regularized GNN Training Objective

The au<sub>g</sub>mented GNN is trained with the objective in Eq. (1):

$$
\begin{array} { r } { \mathcal { L } = \mathcal { L } _ { \mathrm { C E } } + \eta _ { t } \left( \lambda _ { \mathrm { p r o x y } } \mathcal { L } _ { \mathrm { p r o x y } } + \lambda _ { \mathrm { s e p } } \mathcal { L } _ { \mathrm { s e p } } \right. } \\ { \left. + \lambda _ { \mathrm { a n c h o r } } \mathcal { L } _ { \mathrm { a n c h o r } } \right) , \qquad } \end{array}\tag{1}
$$

<sub>w</sub>h<sub>ere</sub> $\eta _ { t } = \operatorname* { m i n } ( 1 , t / T _ { \mathrm { w a r m } } )$ . <sup>H</sup>yp<sup>er</sup>p<sup>arameters</sup> <sup>and</sup> <sup>com</sup>p<sup>o-</sup> n<sub>e</sub>nt <sub>c</sub>h<sub>ec</sub>k<sub>s a</sub>r<sub>e</sub> r<sub>epo</sub>rt<sub>e</sub>d in S<sub>upp</sub>l<sub>e</sub>m<sub>e</sub>nt<sub>a</sub>r<sub>y</sub> S<sub>ec</sub>ti<sub>o</sub>n S3<sub>.</sub>

2019), TAGCN (Du et al. 2018), and GIN (Xu et al. 2019). Th<sub>e</sub> m<sub>a</sub>in <sub>pape</sub>r <sub>co</sub>m<sub>pa</sub>r<sub>es</sub> E<sub>vi-</sub>VN <sub>w</sub>ith<sub>:</sub>

• Backbone only: the first-sta<sub>g</sub>e <sub>g</sub>ra<sub>p</sub>h learner trained on th<sub>e</sub> <sub>or</sub>i<sub>g</sub>i<sub>na</sub>l <sub>grap</sub>h<sub>.</sub>

• GNN probability ensemble: an out<sub>p</sub>ut level ensemble of th<sub>e</sub> 13 fi<sub>rs</sub>t <sub>s</sub>t<sub>age</sub> GNN b<sub>ac</sub>kb<sub>ones.</sub>

• Weighted probability fusion: $( 1 - \lambda ) p _ { i } ^ { G } + \lambda \hat { q } _ { i }$

• Weighted logit fusion: $\sigma ( ( 1 - \lambda ) \log \mathrm { i t } ( p _ { i } ^ { G } ) + \lambda \log \mathrm { i t } ( \hat { q } _ { i } ) )$

## S4 Algorithm and Implementation Details S4.1 Leakage Free Training Checklist

Input. The framework receives graph $G = ( \nu , \mathcal { E } )$ <sub>,</sub> d<sub>a</sub>t<sub>a</sub> <sub>s</sub>id<sub>e</sub> GNN <sub>no</sub>d<sub>e</sub> f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>ures</sub> $\mathbf { X } ^ { G }$ <sub>, ev</sub>id<sub>ence c</sub>h<sub>a</sub>i<sub>n</sub> f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>ures</sub> $\mathbf { S } ^ { E }$ train/dev/test s<sub>p</sub>lits, real node labels on T , <sub>g</sub>ra<sub>p</sub>h learner famil<sub>y</sub> F, router famil<sub>y</sub> G, and <sub>p</sub>rior <sub>g</sub>enerator famil<sub>y</sub> H.

Router and prior generation. Consensus hard labels are <sub>cons</sub>t<sub>ruc</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d f<sub>rom</sub> l<sub>ea</sub>k<sub>age</sub> f<sub>ree mu</sub>lti b<sub>ac</sub>kb<sub>one</sub> GNN OOF <sub>pre</sub>di<sub>c</sub>ti<sub>ons on</sub> th<sub>e</sub> t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>n</sub>i<sub>ng sp</sub>lit<sub>.</sub> Th<sub>e</sub> h<sub>ar</sub>d/<sub>easy rou</sub>t<sub>er</sub> $g _ { \psi }$ <sub>es</sub>ti<sub>ma</sub>t<sub>es</sub> th<sub>ese</sub> l<sub>a</sub>b<sub>e</sub>l<sub>s</sub> f<sub>rom</sub> $\mathbf { s } _ { i } ^ { \bar { E } }$ <sub>.</sub> T<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>n</sub>i<sub>ng no</sub>d<sub>e rou</sub>t<sub>er scores</sub> <sub>are pro</sub>d<sub>uce</sub>d <sub>ou</sub>t <sub>o</sub>f f<sub>o</sub>ld <sub>an</sub>d <sub>ca</sub>lib<sub>ra</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>on</sub> t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>n</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> d<sub>a</sub>t<sub>a on</sub>l<sub>y;</sub> d<sub>eve</sub>l<sub>opmen</sub>t <sub>an</sub>d t<sub>es</sub>t <sub>scores are pro</sub>d<sub>uce</sub>d b<sub>y</sub> th<sub>e</sub> f<sub>u</sub>ll t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>n</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> <sub>rou</sub>t<sub>er an</sub>d th<sub>e same ca</sub>lib<sub>ra</sub>ti<sub>on map.</sub> Th<sub>e rou</sub>t<sub>e mas</sub>k $m _ { i }$ determines whether node i enters the prior generator. Easy route no<sup>d</sup>es <sup>b</sup><sub>yp</sub>ass $h _ { \phi }$ <sub>an</sub>d <sub>rece</sub>i<sub>ve</sub> $\hat { q } _ { i } = 0 . 5$ <sub>.</sub> C<sub>on</sub>diti<sub>ona</sub>l <sub>on</sub> thi<sub>s</sub> <sub>rou</sub>t<sub>e</sub> <sub>mas</sub>k<sub>,</sub> $h _ { \phi }$ i<sub>s</sub> t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>ne</sub>d <sub>on</sub> <sub>rou</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d h<sub>ar</sub>d t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>n</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> <sub>samp</sub>l<sub>es</sub> t<sub>o pre</sub>di<sub>c</sub>t th<sub>e</sub> t<sub>as</sub>k l<sub>a</sub>b<sub>e</sub>l $y _ { i } ,$ <sub>no</sub>t th<sub>e</sub> h<sub>ar</sub>d/<sub>easy</sub> l<sub>a</sub>b<sub>e</sub>l<sub>.</sub>

Virtual nodes and edges. Imputation and standardizati<sub>on are</sub> fitt<sub>e</sub>d <sub>on</sub>l<sub>y on</sub> t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>n</sub>i<sub>ng rea</sub>l <sub>no</sub>d<sub>e</sub> d<sub>a</sub>t<sub>a-s</sub>id<sub>e</sub> f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>ures.</sub> T<sub>wo</sub> fi<sub>xe</sub>d <sub>v</sub>i<sub>r</sub>t<sub>ua</sub>l <sub>no</sub>d<sub>e</sub> f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>ures are cons</sub>t<sub>ruc</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d f<sub>rom</sub> t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>n-</sub> i<sub>ng se</sub>t <sub>c</sub>l<sub>ass pro</sub>t<sub>o</sub>t<sub>ypes w</sub>ith <sub>appen</sub>d<sub>e</sub>d id<sub>en</sub>tit<sub>y</sub> di<sub>mens</sub>i<sub>ons.</sub> E<sub>v</sub>id<sub>ence-c</sub>h<sub>a</sub>i<sub>n</sub> f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>ures are never appen</sub>d<sub>e</sub>d t<sub>o</sub> th<sub>e</sub> GNN <sub>no</sub>d<sub>e</sub> f<sub>ea</sub>t<sub>ure ma</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>x.</sub> A <sub>rou</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d h<sub>ar</sub>d <sub>no</sub>d<sub>e rece</sub>i<sub>ves a source-on</sub>l<sub>y</sub> <sub>e</sub>d<sub>ge</sub> f<sub>rom</sub> th<sub>e pos</sub>iti<sub>ve v</sub>i<sub>r</sub>t<sub>ua</sub>l <sub>c</sub>l<sub>ass no</sub>d<sub>e</sub> if $\hat { q } _ { i }$ i<sub>s a</sub>b<sub>ove</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>pos</sub>iti<sub>ve</sub> th<sub>res</sub>h<sub>o</sub>ld <sub>an</sub>d f<sub>rom</sub> th<sub>e nega</sub>ti<sub>ve v</sub>i<sub>r</sub>t<sub>ua</sub>l <sub>c</sub>l<sub>ass no</sub>d<sub>e</sub> if $\hat { q } _ { i }$ is below the negative threshold. Priors near 0.5 produce weak or no structural injection.

Augmented GNN and finalizer.
